(ii) Model rankings reverse across budgets on all benchmarks ($p {<} 0.01$, McNemar). (iii) Oracle analysis reveals model complementarity up to $+27.8$pp, most pronounced at constrained budgets. (iv) A budget-aware router captures 14.1% of the oracle gap cross-domain; budget features help within-domain ($+1.6$ to $+5.7$pp) but are domain-specific and hurt transfer ($-1.2$pp). These results argue for budget-conditioned evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [231] [How to Spend Your Oracle Budget: Practical Guidance for Protein Structure Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12192)
*Aleksandra Kalisz,Jack Simons,Krisztina Sinkovics,Noam Ghenassia,Shikha Surana,Henry Moss,Paul Duckworth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统比较了蛋白质结构预测中受限于oracle预算的多种引导方法（FK-steering、DPO、Best K-of-N和O3），在calmodulin和E. coli ATCase两个靶标上的实验表明，没有单一方法能在所有预算和oracle下占优：低预算时O3最优，高预算时FK-steering和DPO更佳，并据此为实践者提供了预算感知的方法选择建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质结构预测的基础模型在部分靶标上仍不可靠，外部oracle可以标记并修正这些失败，但生物oracle成本高昂，使oracle预算成为关键约束。现有引导方法（FK-steering、DPO、Best K-of-N）在预算消耗方式上各异，但缺乏系统比较来指导方法选择。

Method: 将近期提出的Optimisation Over Outputs (O3)方法扩展到蛋白质结构预测场景，即在生成模型的潜在子空间内应用现成的优化器。系统基准测试了O3与FK-steering、DPO、Best K-of-N等引导方法，在两个蛋白质靶标（calmodulin 1CLL和E. coli ATCase 9EEH）上进行了评估，考察不同oracle预算设置下的性能表现。

Result: 在两种蛋白质靶标上的评估显示，没有单一方法能在所有oracle预算和oracle类型下持续占优。O3在低oracle预算下表现最佳，而FK-steering和DPO在预算增加时展现出更好的性能改进。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了首个面向oracle预算感知引导的实用参考基准，并将发现提炼为针对具体预算约束下实践者的可操作建议。

Abstract: Foundation models for protein structure prediction remain unreliable on certain targets. External oracles can flag and correct these failures, but biological oracles are expensive, making oracle budget a critical constraint. Existing guidance methods, such as FK-steering, DPO, and Best K-of-N sampling, differ in how they spend this budget, yet no systematic comparison exists to guide method selection. To bridge this gap, we benchmark these methods alongside the recently proposed Optimisation Over Outputs (O3), which applies off-the-shelf optimisers within a generative model's latent subspace. We extend the usage of O3 to protein structure prediction models. Overall, our work provides the first practical reference for oracle budget-aware guidance. Our evaluation on two protein targets, calmodulin (1CLL) and E. coli aspartate transcarbamoylase (9EEH), reveals that no single method consistently dominates across all budgets and oracles. Specifically, O3 proves most effective at low oracle budgets, while FK-steering and DPO demonstrate improved performance as the budget increases. We distil these findings into actionable recommendations for practitioners operating under real-world oracle-budget constraints.

</details>


### [232] [An Agentic Workflow for Legacy HPC Modernization: Converting the Two-Electron-Integral Core of GAMESS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12249)
*Yuzhong Shen,Masha Sosonkina,Peng Xu,Mark S. Gordon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体工作流的大规模Fortran代码现代化方法，通过三个提示专项化的智能体角色在版本控制规范下协作，以人类控制少量门禁点，并通过领域继承的精确验证预言机保证安全。在GAMESS量子化学软件的案例研究中，成功将56,448行、225个子程序的固定形式Fortran 77代码转换为自由形式Fortran 2008，所有12个源文件通过51项测试，612次测试运行中化学相关差异为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留Fortran代码现代化的核心难题是规模问题——单个变换通常很简单，但代码库可能极其庞大，且在许多计算科学领域中这类工作长期被搁置。作者希望将这种规模化工作委托给智能体，并量化这种委托的边界在哪里。

Method: 提出一个智能体工作流：三个提示专项化的代理角色（Claude Code）在隔离的worktree中运行，受版本控制的规范约束（该规范由智能体自行编写和修订），人类仅持有少量门禁点。安全性由领域继承的精确验证预言机（即GAMESS标准测试套件的逐位能量对比）保证，第12位小数的偏差即视为失败。工作跨越四代Claude模型。

Result: 在GAMESS案例研究中，三个智能体角色成功将12个源文件、56,448行代码、225个子程序从Fortran 77转换为Fortran 2008。所有文件通过51项测试（49项标准GAMESS测试加2项额外计算），612次测试运行中化学相关差异为零，所有文件也通过Jenkins持续集成测试。

Conclusion: 安全委托的边界恰好位于领域验证预言机的可见范围之内——只要验证手段能够精确检测差异，智能体就可以在规模化生产中将这类繁琐的代码现代化工作委托出去；超出预言机可见范围的部分则构成委托的安全边界。

Abstract: Modernizing legacy Fortran is a problem of volume: the transformations are individually routine, but the codebases can be enormous, and across much of computational science the work simply goes undone. We propose an agentic workflow that takes this work on at production scale, and we set out to measure how far such delegation can reach. In this work, three prompt-specialized agent roles operate under a version-controlled specification that the agents themselves authored and revised, while humans hold a small number of gates. The arrangement is kept safe by an exact verification oracle inherited from the domain, and the boundary of safe delegation lies exactly where that oracle stops seeing.
  We apply the proposed workflow in a case study, converting the two-electron-integral routines of GAMESS (General Atomic and Molecular Electronic Structure System), a mature quantum-chemistry package with a 48-year development history, from fixed-form Fortran 77 to free-form Fortran 2008. The scope of this work was twelve source files, 56,448 lines, and 225 subroutines for computing electron repulsion integrals. The agents ran as three Claude Code roles in isolated worktrees, and the work spanned four Claude model generations. Because the GAMESS group ships a standard test suite whose printed energies its user community treats as canonical, we could adopt bit-for-bit reproduction of those energies as the merge criterion, where a deviation in the twelfth decimal place counts as a failure rather than drift. All twelve source files pass a 51-test validation battery comprising the 49 standard GAMESS tests and two additional calculations, and across 612 test runs the number of chemistry-relevant differences is zero, and every file also passes the Jenkins tests that are used for continuous integration.

</details>


### [233] [VAKRA: Evaluating Multi-Hop Reasoning Across APIs and Retrieval Under Tool-Use Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12282)
*Ankita Rajaram Naik,Anupama Murthi,Benjamin Elder,Siyu Huo,Raavi Gupta,Abhinav Jain,Praveen Venkateswaran,Abdulhamid Adebayo,Danish Contractor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VAKRA是一个评估企业级Agent在结构化API和文档知识检索场景中推理能力的基准测试，包含62个领域的8000多个可执行API，及三个难度递增的任务设置。研究发现即使是最好模型在组合API任务上准确率也仅约50%，语言中介推理（实体消歧、跨源对齐）是主要失败点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么评估结构化API推理、要么评估文档检索，但企业场景中需要同时具备两者能力。缺乏同时涵盖API交互和知识检索的综合基准，无法真实反映企业级Agent的实际能力。

Method: 构建VAKRA基准，包含8000多个可执行API覆盖62个领域，设计三个递进难度的任务设置：多样化API交互风格、结构化API上的多跳推理、以及带自然语言工具使用策略约束的多源推理。通过重新执行预测的工具调用对实时API验证正确性，并允许多个有效路径。采用固定ReAct框架隔离模型能力与Agent架构，评估前沿和开源模型。

Result: 最好模型在单跳端点任务仅达70.4%准确率，组合API任务降至50-51%；推理深度增加导致性能下降超50%；策略约束的不可回答问题准确率低至2.4%。轨迹分析显示失败集中在语言中介推理（实体消歧和跨源grounding），而非工具调用机制。

Conclusion: 企业级Agent在语言中介推理方面存在重大短板，尤其是实体消歧和跨源信息grounding，而非工具执行本身。这指出了未来Agent能力提升的关键方向，且开源了代码和数据集以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Agents deployed in enterprise settings must reason across structured APIs and document collections, yet existing benchmarks evaluate these capabilities in isolation. We introduce VAKRA (e\textbf{V}aluating \textbf{A}PI and \textbf{K}nowledge \textbf{R}etrieval \textbf{A}gents), a benchmark of over $8{,}000$ executable APIs across $62$ domains with tasks spanning three settings of increasing difficulty: diverse API interaction styles, multi-hop reasoning over structured APIs, and multi-source reasoning with natural-language tool-use policy constraints. Correctness is verified by re-executing predicted tool calls against live APIs, accommodating multiple valid paths. Using a fixed ReAct harness to isolate model capabilities from agent architecture, we evaluate frontier and open-weight models and find that even the best model achieves only 70.4\% on single-hop endpoint-style tasks and drops to 50--51\% on compositional APIs; performance degrades by over 50\% as reasoning depth increases, and policy-constrained questions expose severe failures (as low as 2.4\% on unanswerable queries). Trace analysis shows failures concentrate at language-mediated reasoning - entity disambiguation, cross-source grounding, rather than tool invocation mechanics. Code is available https://github.com/IBM/VAKRA. Dataset is available https://huggingface.co/datasets/ibm-research/VAKRA

</details>


### [234] [Constructing Dynamic Master Logic Models as Knowledge Graphs for Complex System Diagnostics Using Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12304)
*Saman Marandi,Yu-Shu Hu,Mohammad Modarres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用检索增强生成(RAG)和大语言模型(LLM)从系统描述中自动构建动态主逻辑(DML)模型并将其表示为知识图谱(KG-DML)的框架，并将该框架从先前的小规模系统扩展到更大型复杂系统。以退役沸水反应堆的低压冷却剂注入系统为案例，验证了多层级验证方法下的模型重构一致性与结构完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DML提供了一种将功能目标与底层结构要素关联的分层框架来表征系统行为，但传统的DML构建依赖专家对技术文档的解读，难以扩展到复杂系统。需要一种自动化的构建方法以提升可扩展性和效率。

Method: 采用检索增强生成(RAG)与大语言模型(LLM)作为工具，从系统描述中自动构建DML模型并表示为知识图谱(KG-DML)。构建过程沿DML层级进行定向检索，同时保留功能依赖关系和显式逻辑关系。验证采用多层级的评估方法，涵盖层次特定的精确率与召回率、逻辑门一致性以及整体结构完整性。

Result: 将该框架应用于退役沸水反应堆的低压冷却剂注入系统，多次重复运行下模型重构保持一致。结果表明，自动化KG-DML构建能将技术文档转化为可用于诊断和可靠性分析的可执行功能模型。

Conclusion: 自动化KG-DML构建方法可行且稳健，能够有效将技术文档转化为可执行的系统功能模型，支持诊断推理、安全评估、向上故障传播与向下依赖追踪等多种分析任务，为复杂系统的功能建模提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Dynamic Master Logic (DML) provides a hierarchical framework for representing system behavior by linking functional objectives to underlying structural elements. However, DML construction typically relies on expert interpretation of technical documentation, limiting scalability for complex systems. This study presents a framework for automated construction of DML models from system descriptions and their representation as Knowledge Graphs (KG-DML), using Retrieval-Augmented Generation and Large Language Models as enabling tools. Building on prior work with small-scale systems, the framework extends automated KG-DML construction and evaluation to substantially larger and more complex systems. Model construction proceeds across the DML hierarchy using targeted retrieval while preserving functional dependencies and explicit logical relationships. The resulting KG-DML supports diagnostic reasoning, safety assessment, upward failure propagation, and downward dependency tracing. A multi-level validation methodology evaluates layer-specific precision and recall, logical gate consistency, and overall structural integrity. Application to the Low-Pressure Coolant Injection system of a decommissioned Boiling Water Reactor demonstrates consistent reconstruction across repeated runs. The results show that automated KG-DML construction can transform technical documentation into executable functional models for diagnostic and reliability analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [235] [FarSky: Task-Aware Latent-Space Coupling for Generative Intra-Hour Solar Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11254)
*Yann Fabel,Bijan Nouri,Milon Miah,Niklas Blum,Luis F. Zarzalejo,Julia Kowalski,Robert Pitz-Paal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FarSky是一个基于潜空间耦合的生成式太阳辐照度预测框架，通过多任务自编码器学习天空图像的共享潜表示，再利用潜扩散模型生成未来潜状态并解码为辐照度预测。该方法在西班牙Almería太阳平台的多年度全天候天空成像仪数据集上，相比持续法、端到端方法和生成式方法，实现了最佳的整体确定性与概率性预测性能，预测技能提升高达11个百分点，斜坡事件检测F1分数超过60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的太阳辐照度预测对光伏发电可靠并网至关重要。现有深度学习方法在短期预测中虽有显著提升，但受限于确定性预测以及对斜坡事件（ramp events）预判能力不足的问题，亟需一种能够提供概率性预测并更好应对天气突变的生成式建模方案。

Method: FarSky由两个核心模块组成：(1) 多任务自编码器，通过联合训练图像重建与辐照度估计任务，学习任务感知（task-aware）的共享潜空间表示；(2) 潜扩散模型（latent diffusion model），基于近期观测条件生成未来潜状态，并通过随机采样天然获得概率性预测，最后从潜状态直接解码出辐照度预测值。

Result: 在Plataforma Solar de Almería的多年度ASI数据集上，使用两个独立测试集进行验证，FarSky在整体确定性预测和概率性预测方面均达到最优性能，预报技能提升最多达11个百分点；在斜坡事件检测方面，F1分数超过60%，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: FarSky验证了将生成式模型与任务感知的潜空间耦合相结合用于太阳辐照度预测的有效性，不仅提升了预测精度，还显著增强了斜坡事件的捕获能力，为光伏功率预测提供了一种兼具确定性与概率性输出的新范式。

Abstract: Accurate solar irradiance forecasting is essential for the reliable integration of photovoltaic power into modern electricity grids. All-sky imagers (ASI) provide high-resolution observations of clouds, making them well suited for intra-hour forecasting. Recent deep learning approaches have substantially improved forecast accuracy but are often limited by deterministic predictions and a reduced capability to anticipate ramp events. This work proposes FarSky, a generative forecasting framework that leverages latent-space coupling to learn task-aware representations of sky images. A multi-task autoencoder first learns a shared latent representation for image reconstruction and irradiance estimation. A latent diffusion model then generates future latent states conditioned on recent observations, from which irradiance forecasts are directly decoded. Probabilistic forecasts are inherently obtained through stochastic sampling. The framework is developed using a multi-year ASI dataset acquired at the Plataforma Solar de Almería, Spain, and evaluated on two independent test datasets against persistence, state-of-the-art end-to-end, and generative forecasting approaches. FarSky achieves the best overall deterministic and probabilistic forecasting performance, improving forecast skill by up to 11 percentage points. Furthermore, it substantially improves ramp event detection over existing methods, achieving F1-scores above 60%. These results demonstrate the potential of combining generative models with task-aware latent-space coupling for solar forecasting.

</details>


### [236] [Why AI Detection Fails for Academic Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11256)
*Jonathan A. Karr,Grigorii Khvatskii,Ting Hua,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过对照实验（2013-2015年 vs 2023-2025年发表的英文摘要，涵盖四个领域）量化了商业AI检测器无法区分轻度AI编辑与完整LLM草稿的问题：符合指南的轻度"仅润色摘要"编辑被判定为违规的比例高达64-80%，而未经修改的原稿在2023-2025年样本中有9-15%被误判；经"Undetectable AI"人性化处理后，检测规避近乎完全（检出率<4%）。揭示诚实使用AI辅助编辑比借助人性化工具规避检测面临更高的处罚风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构普遍使用商业AI检测器作为学术诚信工具，但检测器无法区分AI编辑与完整LLM草稿，可能将两类情况都视为学术不端。作者旨在量化这种政策失效的程度，评估检测器在实际场景中的可靠性。

Method: 采用受控研究设计，选取四个领域的已发表英文摘要，分为2013-2015年（人类作者代理标签）和2023-2025年（AI时代）两个时段，设置tau=0.50的代理阈值；使用两种商业检测器（Pangram和GPTZero）检测；通过"仅润色摘要"的轻编辑模拟符合指南的AI辅助；使用Undetectable AI进行人性化处理测试规避效果；并分析长token密度和学术词表（AWL）密度与检测分数的关联。

Result: 轻度AI编辑被标记率为64-80%；未经修改的2023-2025年原稿误判率为9-15%，其中非STEM领域误判率显著高于STEM（p<0.001）；高检测分数与长token及学术词表密度相关而非仅由作者意图决定；经Undetectable AI人性化后，AI标记的改写文本残留标记率低于4%，漏检率（FNR）超过96%。

Conclusion: 检测器分数不应作为独立的学术不端证据。当前检测体系存在系统性缺陷：诚实使用AI辅助编辑的学者面临比使用规避工具者更高的处罚风险，这是政策的严重不公平。学术机构应重新审视依赖AI检测器进行完整性判断的做法。

Abstract: Institutions use commercial AI detectors for academic integrity, yet detectors cannot distinguish AI editing from full LLM drafts and may treat both as misconduct. In a controlled study of published English abstracts (four domains; 2013 to 2015 vs. 2023 to 2025), we quantify this policy failure under proxy human/AI labels at tau=0.50. Light "refine abstract only" edits, a proxy for guideline-compliant AI assistance, are flagged at 64 to 80% (Pangram/GPTZero). Unmodified 2023 to 2025 originals are flagged at 9 to 15%, with non-STEM rates far above STEM (p<0.001); elevated scores track long-token and Academic Word List density, not authorship intent alone. After Undetectable AI humanization, evasion is near-total: fewer than 4% of AI-labeled rewrites remain flagged (post-humanization detection rate <4%; FNR >96%). Honest AI-editing results in a higher sanction risk than humanizer-assisted evasion. Therefore, detector scores should not serve as standalone misconduct evidence.

</details>


### [237] [Basin: Efficient and Extensible Numerical Optimization in Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11279)
*Johan Larsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Basin是一个面向Rust编程语言的数值优化库，提供统一的接口来定义和求解各类最小化问题，涵盖丰富的求解器目录并对约束条件提供一流支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值优化是科学计算中的基础任务，广泛应用于模型拟合、模拟校准、机器学习训练和工程参数优化等领域。然而在Rust生态中缺乏一个统一、一致的方式来表述和求解这类问题，Basin旨在填补这一空白。

Method: Basin作为一个Rust数值优化库，为用户提供统一的接口来陈述和求解优化问题，内置广泛的求解器目录，并对约束条件提供一流的（first-class）支持。

Result: 该文段为论文引言/摘要部分，尚未提供具体的实验结果或性能评估数据。

Conclusion: Basin为Rust社区提供了一个统一且一致的数值优化框架，解决了优化问题表述和求解的标准化需求。

Abstract: Basin is a numerical optimization library for the Rust programming language. Numerical optimization is the task of finding the inputs that minimize a function, and it is a fundamental element across the sciences: fitting a model to data, calibrating a simulation, training a machine learning model, or choosing engineering parameters that minimize cost. Basin gives users a single, consistent way to both state and solve such problems, with a broad catalog of solvers and first-class support for constraints.

</details>


### [238] [Federated Learning for Distributed CNC Tool Wear Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11281)
*Afsana Khan,Morris Stallmann,Marcin Pietrasik,Charis Kouzinopoulos,Anna Wilbik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在CNC加工中利用联邦学习进行刀具磨损预测的方法。刀具轨迹数据分布在模拟客户端上，联邦模型与集中式参考模型和本地客户端基线进行了比较，结果表明联邦学习性能接近集中式学习且显著优于本地模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刀具磨损预测对CNC加工的产品质量和工艺可靠性至关重要，但机器学习方法在工业环境中受限于数据的分布式特性以及机器、站点或组织间的数据共享限制。需要一个能在不传输原始运行数据的情况下进行协作训练的框架。

Method: 采用联邦学习框架进行CNC刀具磨损预测。将刀具轨迹数据分布在模拟客户端上以模拟联邦学习场景，并将联邦模型与集中式训练的参考模型以及各客户端本地训练的基线模型进行对比评估。

Result: 联邦学习达到了接近集中式学习的性能，并且相比本地客户端模型有显著改进。

Conclusion: 联邦学习能够支持分布式CNC制造环境中的协作刀具磨损预测，在保护数据隐私的同时保持模型性能。

Abstract: Tool wear prediction is an important task in CNC machining, where accurate monitoring of tool condition supports product quality and process reliability. Machine learning methods have shown potential for this task, but their use in industrial environments is limited by the distributed nature of machining data and by restrictions on data sharing between machines, sites, or organizations. Federated learning offers a suitable framework for this setting by enabling collaborative model training without transferring raw operational data. This paper investigates federated learning for CNC tool wear prediction. Tool trajectories are distributed across simulated clients to represent a federated learning scenario. The federated models are compared against centralized references and local client baselines. Results show that federated learning achieves performance close to centralized learning and improves significantly over local client models. These findings indicate that federated learning can support collaborative tool wear prediction in distributed CNC manufacturing environments.

</details>


### [239] [Terminal Symmetry as a Decision Resource: Statewise Refinement for Anytime Verified Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11318)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"终止对称性"的决策-资源视角，将序列化构建任务的终止对称性分解为传输-细化-验证（transport-refine-certify）机制，并实例化为方法\method{}，在CAD装配、小程序、精确填充等多领域任务中显著提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多序列化构建任务在完成时具有精确对称性，但执行过程是定向且历史依赖的。现有的方法尚未系统地将这种终止对称性作为可复用的决策资源加以利用。作者希望建立一种统一的视角，将终止对称性转化为可操作的决策信号，提升构建任务的规划效率与性能。

Method: \method{}包含四个核心组件：(1) 一个episode固定的传输过程结构；(2) 状态受限的过程秩（state-restricted process rank）；(3) 在接受的转换后刷新的状态依赖残差秩（state-dependent residual rank）；(4) 一个序数秩取交（ordinal rank meet）操作，其top-k集合恰好是两种提议前缀的并集。该取交操作在前缀覆盖条件下提供完成保证，并在对应前缀信息模型下达到最紧的验证器查询上界。

Result: 状态级刷新在CAD装配、小程序、精确填充任务中分别将任意时刻AUC提升6.77、21.75和8.68个百分点。在GRN OOD场景的1,135个目标移除episodes上，\method{}在三个尺度上均取得最低的平均封顶验证器成本。状态信号在聚合与调度器组织间具有迁移性。

Conclusion: 终止对称性可以被系统性地分解并作为可复用的决策资源，为定向序列化构建任务提供统一的决策框架与性能增益。两状态构建实验进一步预测了转换后存在严格的动态-静态分离现象，揭示了该方法在时序决策中的结构优势。

Abstract: Many sequential construction tasks exhibit exact symmetry at completion while their execution remains directed and history-dependent. We develop a decision-resource view of terminal symmetry: process evidence supplies directionality, terminal correspondence transports that structure across equivalent outcomes, realized-state evidence refines its current decision relevance after transitions, and a fixed verifier certifies execution. This decomposition yields transport--refine--certify. \method{} instantiates the principle with an episode-fixed transported process structure, its state-restricted process rank, a state-dependent residual rank refreshed after accepted transitions, and an ordinal rank meet whose top-$k$ set is exactly the union of the two proposal prefixes. The meet provides a completion guarantee under prefix coverage and attains the tight worst-case verifier-query bound under the corresponding prefix information model; a two-state construction predicts a strict post-transition dynamic--static separation. Across CAD assembly, Mini-Programs, and exact-fill packing, statewise refresh improves anytime AUC by up to $6.77$, $21.75$, and $8.68$ points, respectively. On 1,135 target-removal episodes from the official GRN OOD scenes, \method{} attains the lowest mean capped verifier cost at all three scales among the compared GRN and CDGS-style planners. The statewise signal also transfers across aggregation and scheduler organizations. Terminal symmetry thereby becomes a reusable decision resource for directed construction.

</details>


### [240] [Contextual Quality-Diversity Evolutionary Reinforcement Learning for HVAC Control in Tropical Commercial Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11324)
*Tran Le Vu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于上下文质量-多样性进化强化学习控制器（CQD-ERL），用于热带水冷式冷水机组及其风侧的监督控制。该控制器不收敛于单一的标量化策略，而是维护一个按数据驱动的运行上下文（日常天气和负荷模式聚类）及上下文不变的行为描述符联合索引的产品档案库，由无梯度进化算子和软演员-评论家策略梯度算子共享一个回放缓冲区共同填充，每个动作在执行前都经过确定性安全屏蔽过滤。控制器在代表新加坡商业建筑潜在负荷、冷却塔逼近度和湿度约束的两层降阶环境上训练，并在全年度回测中与ASHRAE指南36基线进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑暖通空调系统的监督控制面临多目标、多模态最优解的挑战，传统单策略强化学习方法难以在复杂运行环境中保持鲁棒性和多样性，需要一种能够同时探索多种可行控制策略并适应不同运行上下文的控制方案。

Method: 采用质量-多样性进化与软演员-评论家（SAC）策略梯度相结合的混合方法，通过共享回放缓冲区协同优化；维护一个由运行上下文聚类和行为描述符联合索引的产品档案库；引入确定性安全屏蔽保障执行安全。

Result: 在全年度回测中与ASHRAE Guideline 36基线进行对比评估，验证了CQD-ERL控制器在热带水冷式冷水机组场景下的控制性能。

Conclusion: CQD-ERL通过上下文感知的质量-多样性进化强化学习框架，实现了对复杂建筑暖通空调系统的高效监督控制，在保持策略多样性的同时兼顾了安全性与节能性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes a contextual quality-diversity evolutionary reinforcement-learning controller, CQD-ERL, for the supervisory control of a tropical, water-cooled chiller plant and its associated air side. Rather than converging to a single scalarised policy, the controller maintains a product archive of specialised policies indexed jointly by a data- driven operating context, a cluster of daily weather and load regime, and a context-invariant behaviour descriptor, filled by a gradient-free evolutionary operator and a soft-actor-critic policy-gradient operator that share one replay buffer. Every action is filtered through a deterministic safety shield before execution. The controller is trained on a two-tier reduced-order environment representing the latent load, cooling-tower approach and humidity constraints of a Singapore commercial building, and is evaluated over a full annual backtest against an ASHRAE Guideline 36 baseline.

</details>


### [241] [Long-Horizon Forecasting of Complete Financial Statements with Forma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11327)
*Travis L. Johnson,Jiannan Jiang,Soumyabrata Chaudhuri,Yihao Chen,Lauren Falvey,Donal O'Cofaigh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProForma-20Q基准和Forma模型，用于联合预测1-20个季度的完整财务报表（78个科目），Forma通过将报表表示为(account, quarter, value)元组集合并以掩码元组高斯似然训练，在多个对比基准（经典ML、梯度提升、时序基础模型、前沿LLM）中均取得最优表现，且领先优势随预测时域扩大而增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对完整财务报表的联合预测（尤其超过一年的跨时域预测），而贴现现金流估值中大部分企业价值恰恰落在该窗口之外。因此需要构建可复现的基准和专用模型来填补这一空白。

Method: （1）构建ProForma-20Q基准：包含78个报表科目、预测1-20个季度、基于匿名公司、输入为历史报表与行业代码、评估指标为变化空间R²；（2）提出Forma模型：一个transformer，将报表表示为(account, quarter, value)元组集合，通过最大化掩码元组高斯似然进行训练——即随机掩码部分元组并回归其值及不确定性。

Result: Forma在所有对比方法中表现最佳，击败经典机器学习、链式梯度提升、零样本时序基础模型及前沿大语言模型；其领先优势随预测时域延长而扩大（恰是估值最需要准确性的区域）；高斯预测区间从不欠覆盖；预测几乎满足会计恒等式，且可无损恢复精确一致性；支持无需重训练的情景分析，固定未来收入路径可提高报表其余部分的预测精度。

Conclusion: 专业化的报表预测模型（Forma）优于通用的规模化模型，其元组接口设计兼具预测精度、不确定性校准、会计一致性可恢复性与情景分析灵活性，为财务报表长期联合预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Specialist training beats generalist scale when forecasting financial statements. To our knowledge, no prior work jointly forecasts complete financial statements beyond one year, yet in a discounted-cash-flow valuation most firm value sits past that window. We release ProForma-20Q, a reproducible benchmark for forecasting 78 statement line items 1-20 quarters ahead, for anonymized firms, from past statements and an industry code, scored by change-space $R^2$. On it, Forma, a transformer that reads statements as sets of (account, quarter, value) tuples and maximizes a masked-tuple Gaussian likelihood, beats every competitor we field: classical machine learning, chained gradient boosting, a zero-shot time-series foundation model, and frontier large language models. Its lead widens with horizon, where valuation needs accuracy most, and its Gaussian predictive intervals never under-cover. Forma's forecasts nearly satisfy accounting identities; exact coherence is recoverable at no statistically significant accuracy cost. Its tuple interface supports scenario analysis without retraining, and we show that pinning future revenue paths sharpens the rest of the statement.

</details>


### [242] [Weightless Fine-Tuning: Personalizing LLMs via Logit-Space Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11342)
*Bohan Zhang,Anqi Ni,Yixin Wang,Paramveer S. Dhillon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT)，一种无需权重更新的训练-free解码时方法，通过跨前缀传输算子模拟SFT的分布效应，在个性化任务中以不足7%的计算成本达到接近SFT的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFT是适配LLM到目标分布的标准方法，但在个性化等场景中，每个作者都需要独立的权重访问、优化、存储和重训练，成本过高。因此需要一种无需更新模型权重但仍能近似SFT分布效果的轻量级方法。

Method: WFT在解码时计算作者训练序列上的监督残差，并通过从dropout诱导的跨协方差中估计的跨前缀传输算子，将这些残差传输到当前提示。该算子捕捉一个上下文中的扰动如何传播到另一上下文的预测，用logit空间校正替代基于梯度的参数更新。

Result: 在三个LaMP个性化基准上，WFT取得最佳平均性能，在个别任务上匹配或超越SFT，并平均超越其他轻量级基线。在预算控制对比中，WFT以不到7%的有效计算量接近SFT性能。Logit级分析显示WFT与SFT诱导的logit偏移余弦相似度为0.875（覆盖95%的next-token概率质量）。

Conclusion: WFT在不修改模型权重的情况下捕获了监督适配的分布效应，证明训练-free的解码时方法可以成为SFT在个性化场景中的高效替代方案。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is a standard approach for adapting LLMs to a target distribution, but in settings such as personalization, where each author requires separate weight access, optimization, storage, and retraining, its costs become prohibitive. We propose Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT), a training-free decoding-time method that approximates the distributional effect of SFT without weight updates. WFT computes supervised residuals on an author's training sequence and transports them to the current prompt through a cross-prefix transport operator estimated from dropout-induced cross-covariance. The operator captures how a perturbation at one context propagates to predictions at another, replacing gradient-based parameter updates with logit-space corrections. On three LaMP personalization benchmarks, WFT achieves the best average performance across datasets, matches or exceeds SFT on individual tasks, and outperforms other lightweight baselines on average. In a budget-controlled comparison, WFT approaches SFT performance using less than 7% of the effective computation. Logit-level analysis shows a cosine similarity of 0.875 between the logit shifts induced by WFT and SFT over 95% of the next-token probability mass, suggesting that WFT captures the distributional effect of supervised adaptation without modifying model weights.

</details>


### [243] [Dynamics Models for Offline Hyperparameter Selection in Real-World RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11349)
*Jordan Coblin,Han Wang,Martha White,Adam White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将校准模型（calibration models）应用于真实工业场景——市政水处理厂，用于离线强化学习超参数选择。在基于拉普拉斯距离的k近邻模型等方法上，对高维非平稳传感器数据进行预测任务评估，证明其能生成逼真的长期轨迹、恢复有意义的超参数敏感性趋势，并检验了模型在年度数据扩展、迁移学习率和分布偏移下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在真实系统部署的核心障碍之一是超参数选择，尤其在无模拟器且在线实验成本高昂的情况下。先前基于离线数据训练的校准模型已被提出用于近似环境动态，但仅在简单仿真环境中验证过，缺乏真实工业场景的实践检验。

Method: 在市政水处理厂这一真实工业环境中，评估多种校准模型方法（包括采用拉普拉斯距离度量的k近邻模型），用于高维、非平稳传感器数据的预测任务；进一步考察模型在长达一年的数据集上的可扩展性、对预训练智能体微调学习率选择的支撑能力，以及在分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Result: 校准模型能够生成真实感强的长期轨迹，并能恢复有意义的超参数敏感性趋势；在年度数据、微调学习率选择以及分布偏移场景下均展现出一定的实用性，但同时也暴露出若干实际挑战。

Conclusion: 本研究为离线动态模型支持真实环境中的强化学习部署提供了概念验证，同时指出了未来工作需解决的重要实践难题。

Abstract: A key obstacle to deploying reinforcement learning in real-world systems is hyperparameter selection, particularly when simulators are unavailable and online experimentation is costly. Prior work has proposed calibration models trained on offline data to approximate environment dynamics and enable offline hyperparameter selection, but these methods have so far been evaluated only in simple simulated settings. In this paper, we present the first application of calibration models in a real-world industrial setting: a municipal water treatment plant. We evaluate several calibration model approaches, including a k-nearest neighbors model with a Laplacian distance metric, on high-dimensional, non-stationary sensor data for nexting prediction tasks. Our results show that these models can generate realistic long-horizon rollouts and recover meaningful hyperparameter sensitivity trends. We further examine how calibration models scale to year-long datasets, how they support the selection of fine-tuning learning rates for pre-trained agents, and how robust they are under distribution shift. Overall, our findings provide a proof of concept for using offline dynamics models to support RL deployment in real-world environments, while highlighting important practical challenges for future work.

</details>


### [244] [Market-Information-Aware Gated-LoRA of Foundation Models for Transferable Day-Ahead Electricity Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11359)
*Hang Fan,Wei Wei,Shengwei Mei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种市场信息感知的适配框架，将Chronos-2时序基础模型迁移到日前电价预测。通过构建多源市场信息接口并结合门控LoRA适配器，仅更新约1%的模型参数即可实现跨市场迁移。在中国四个省级日前现货市场上的实验表明，该框架相比零样本Chronos-2和普通Source-LoRA均有性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电价预测对市场参与者至关重要，但传统监督方法高度依赖特定市场的历史数据，这在新建市场或数据稀缺市场中效果较差。如何将预训练时间序列基础模型有效迁移到目标市场，同时利用市场特有的系统运行条件信息（如供需、储备、检修、机组容量、联络线等），是亟待解决的问题。

Method: 提出市场信息感知适配框架：1）构建多源市场信息（MSMI）接口，将7天价格上下文与预出清供需、储备、检修、机组容量、联络线等变量对齐；2）训练源域门控低秩适配器（gated LoRA），仅更新约1%的模型参数且不需要目标市场标签；3）门控机制根据储备紧张度和运行状态信号对冻结的源适配器进行缩放；4）采用留一市场（leave-one-market-out）协议评估跨市场可迁移性。

Result: 在中国四个省级日前现货市场上的实验表明：相比市场信息感知的零样本Chronos-2，平均MAE/RMSE分别降低6.24%/7.99%；相比普通Source-LoRA，平均MAE/RMSE分别降低3.05%/3.52%。消融实验显示，门控带来的增益无法通过学习全局标量或随机门初始化复现，且相对于Source-LoRA的额外提升有限。

Conclusion: 市场结构化输入和状态依赖的门控LoRA为数据稀缺的电力市场提供了一条实用的跨市场迁移路径，但门控机制带来的额外提升相对有限，表明其在所研究市场中的增量贡献需要进一步权衡。

Abstract: Electricity price forecasting is crucial for market participants but remains difficult because prices are volatile, market-specific, and closely tied to anticipated system conditions. Existing supervised methods depend largely on market-specific historical data, limiting their use in newly established or data-scarce markets. This paper proposes a market-information-aware adaptation framework that transfers the Chronos-2 time-series foundation model to day-ahead electricity price forecasting. It first constructs a multi-source market information (MSMI) interface aligning 7-day price context with pre-clearing supply--demand, reserve, maintenance, generator-capacity, and intertie variables, and then trains a source-domain gated low-rank adapter (LoRA), updating about $1\%$ of model parameters without target-market labels. The gate scales the frozen source adapter according to reserve-tightness and operating-state signals. A leave-one-market-out protocol is adopted for evaluating cross-market transferability. Experiments on four Chinese provincial day-ahead spot markets show that the proposed framework reduces the average MAE/RMSE by $6.24\%/7.99\%$ relative to market-information-aware zero-shot Chronos-2 and by $3.05\%/3.52\%$ relative to vanilla Source-LoRA. Experiments show that the gain is not reproduced by a learned global scalar or by random gate initialization, while the additional improvement over Source-LoRA is limited. These results suggest that market-structured inputs and state-dependent gated LoRA can provide a practical transfer path for data-scarce electricity markets.

</details>


### [245] [Lifecycle-Optimal Tokenization: Vocabulary Size as a Deployment-Regime-Dependent Infrastructure Parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11361)
*Rima Mittal,Ankit Gubrani,Satyanarayana Kakollu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过形式化总部署成本模型和在两种GPU（A10G、A100）上的受控实验，证明词表大小（tokenizer vocabulary size）的最优值不是常数，而是随服务场景（推理批量大小B和推理量λ）变化的函数。结果显示推理最优词表随批量大小从32k（B=1）变化到524k（B=64+），生命周期最优词表与训练最优词表差异可达16倍，且该范围内质量损失极小（<2% BPB），为容量规划提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词表大小是大语言模型基础设施中的基础设计选择，但传统上在训练时基于惯例固定，而非基于部署分析。作者希望揭示词表大小与部署成本之间的系统关系，打破"固定词表"的惯例，提供数据驱动的容量规划依据。

Method: 作者形式化了全生命周期部署成本模型C_lifecycle(V) = C_train(V) + λ·C_infer(V, B)，其中λ为推理量、B为服务批量大小。随后在两种GPU（A10G和A100）上进行受控实验，覆盖从内存受限到计算受限的计算区间（ridge点分别为117和183 FLOP/byte），系统性地变化词表大小并测量训练和推理成本及质量（BPB）。

Result: 主要发现：1) 推理最优词表随批量大小从32k（B=1）到524k（B=64+）变化16倍，由V×d unembedding矩阵读取的摊销驱动；2) 在1.3-2.3B模型规模下，质量最优词表为65k，确认词表偏好随模型规模变化；3) 生命周期最优词表与训练最优词表在生产部署场景下差异可达16倍；4) 优化范围内的质量变化不超过2% BPB，使词表成为一个纯系统优化问题。

Conclusion: 词表大小应根据实际部署场景进行选择而非按惯例固定：端侧部署（B=1）应使用V≈32k；数据中心服务（B≥64，λ≥10）应使用V≈131-262k。由于最优范围内质量损失可忽略，词表成为一个纯粹的系统优化变量，可无质量代价地优化部署成本。

Abstract: Tokenizer vocabulary size is a foundational design choice in large language model (LLM) infrastructure, yet it is typically fixed at training time based on convention rather than deployment analysis. We show that the cost-optimal vocabulary is not a constant but a function of the serving regime. We formalize total deployment cost as $C_{lifecycle}(V) = C_{train}(V) + λ\cdot C_{infer}(V, B)$, where $λ$ is inference volume and $B$ is the serving batch size. Through controlled experiments on two GPU families spanning the memory-bound to compute-bound regimes (A10G, ridge $\approx$ 117 FLOP/byte; A100, ridge $\approx$ 183 FLOP/byte), we demonstrate: (1) the inference-optimal vocabulary shifts 16x with serving batch, from 32k at $B=1$ to 524k at $B=64+$, driven by amortization of the $V \times d$ unembedding matrix read; (2) at 1.3-2.3B model scale, quality (bits per byte, BPB) is optimized at $V=65$k, confirming scale-dependent vocabulary preference; (3) the lifecycle-optimal vocabulary diverges from training-optimal by up to 16x for production deployments. Quality is approximately invariant across the optimal range ($<$2% BPB spread), making vocabulary a pure systems optimization with no quality penalty in the measured range. Our results provide actionable capacity planning guidance: on-device deployments ($B=1$) should use $V \approx 32$k; datacenter serving ($B \geq 64$, $λ\geq 10$) should use $V \approx 131$-262k.

</details>


### [246] [PAIR: Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting for Adaptive Rollout Allocation in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11368)
*Pixel Nomand,Elena Voss,Marcus Hale,Sofia Reyes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PAIR（Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting）方法，解决RLVR中基于逐点难度/效用分配计算预算的统计失配问题。作者发现未裁剪的留一法组相对评分梯度本质是二阶U统计量（对轮次对），而非独立点贡献之和。PAIR将短推理前缀视为顶点、pair梯度项视为对比图的边，通过凸优化选择正向延续概率，并对每条边按其联合包含概率的倒数加权，实现了设计无偏的完整候选对梯度估计。在Qwen3-1.7B/4B上，PAIR相比最强逐点分配器平均准确率提升+1.2/+1.4，同时相比full-group GRPO节省51%/52%的生成token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR计算分配器根据逐点难度或效用为提示、轮次或token分配预算，但其隐含假设——梯度是独立点贡献之和——与真实统计结构不符。未裁剪的留一法组相对评分梯度本质上是成对rollout的二阶U统计量，一个rollout完成后会与所有已完成rollout产生对比，这使得自适应端点选择改变可观测的pair项。这种统计失配导致逐点分配器在理论上存在偏差，需要从对级（pair-level）视角重新设计分配与加权方法。

Method: PAIR方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 将短rollout前缀建模为对比图的顶点，pair梯度项建模为边；(2) 使用前缀专用预测器估计正确性与剩余token成本，通过凸优化在期望后缀token预算下选择正延续概率；(3) 对由已完成顶点诱导的每条边，按其历史联合包含概率的倒数进行逆概率加权。在条件独立on-policy rollout和未裁剪、未标准化目标的假设下，该估计量对完整候选对梯度是设计无偏的。

Result: 在计算量匹配的RLVR实验中（Qwen3-1.7B和4B）：PAIR比最强逐点分配器平均准确率分别提升+1.2和+1.4；相比full-group GRPO节省51%和52%的生成token。冻结种群估计审计证实：未加权的自适应选择存在偏差，而pair包含校正能在匹配后缀成本下恢复完整对目标的估计。

Conclusion: RLVR中的预算分配应从"逐点"范式转向"逐对"范式。PAIR通过在对比图上进行包含重加权，从理论上保证了梯度估计的无偏性，并在实践中同时提升了准确率与token效率，验证了pair-wise统计结构对自适应推理分配的重要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) spends most of its compute generating groups of long reasoning trajectories. Recent allocators reduce this cost by assigning budgets to prompts, rollouts, or tokens according to a pointwise notion of difficulty or utility. We identify a statistical mismatch: the unclipped leave-one-out group-relative score gradient is not a sum of independent point contributions, but a second-order U-statistic over pairs of rollouts. Completing one rollout therefore reveals contrast with every other completed rollout, and adaptive endpoint selection changes which pair terms are observable. We introduce PAIR (Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting), which treats short rollout prefixes as vertices and pair-gradient terms as edges of a contrast graph. A prefix-only predictor estimates correctness and remaining token cost; a convex design chooses positive continuation probabilities under an expected suffix-token budget; and each edge induced by completed vertices is inverse-weighted by its logged joint inclusion probability. Under conditionally independent on-policy rollouts and an unclipped, unstandardized objective, the resulting estimator is design-unbiased for the complete candidate-pair gradient. Across compute-matched RLVR runs on Qwen3-1.7B/4B, PAIR improves average accuracy by +1.2 and +1.4 over the strongest pointwise allocator while using 51% and 52% fewer generated tokens than full-group GRPO. A frozen-population estimator audit confirms that unweighted adaptive selection is biased, whereas pair-inclusion correction recovers the complete-pair target at matched suffix cost.

</details>


### [247] [Towards an approach to multivariate outlier detection for District Heating System data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11375)
*Rajko Turudija,Dušan Stojiljković,Milan Zdravković,Marko Ignjatović*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对区域供热系统变电站的传输热能数据，结合外部环境温度，测试了多种多元离群点检测方法（Z-score、马氏距离、PCA、隔离森林和Hotelling T²检验），通过领域专家讨论评估各方法效果，最终采用三种有效方法（PCA、隔离森林、Hotelling）的集成方法作为最终方案，以识别异常运行并减少天然气消耗和CO2排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在揭示供热厂的不正常运行状态，通过检测数据中的异常点来识别运行效率低下的情况，从而寻找减少天然气消耗和CO2排放的机会。

Method: 测试多种离群点检测方法：Z-score（单变量基准）、马氏距离、主成分分析（PCA）、隔离森林和Hotelling T²检验。方法需考虑特定领域情况（如零传输能量时间点不代表停机）。检测结果由领域专家讨论评估，最终采用基于三种有效方法一致同意的集成方法。

Result: PCA、隔离森林和Hotelling T²检验这三种方法提供了相关且有效的结果，而Z-score和（可能）马氏距离效果不佳。最终采用三种有效方法的集成（agreement-based ensemble）作为最终离群点检测方案。

Conclusion: 采用基于PCA、隔离森林和Hotelling方法一致性的集成方法能有效检测区域供热系统中的异常运行，为优化运行和降低碳排放提供数据支持。

Abstract: In this paper, we test different methods for multivariate detection of outliers in the data of transmitted heat energy in the selected substation of local District Heating System, by also considering outside ambient temperature, namely Z-score (univariate, as a benchmark), Mahalanobis distances, Principal Component Analysis (PCA), Isolation Forest and Hotelling's T-squared test. The overall research aims at uncovering irregular plant operation, with a wider objective of identifying the opportunities for reducing the consumption of gas in central heating plants as well as the CO2 emission. The proposed approach considers specific domain circumstances, such as irrelevance of zero transmit-ted energy timepoints as indication of off-grid plant. The outcomes of the different methods are discussed with domain experts. It was concluded that PCA, Isolation Forest and Hotelling method provide relevant results. Finally, we adopt the ensemble method (selection based on the agreement of all three methods on the detected outliers) as the final approach.

</details>


### [248] [Reoptimization Algorithms for Contextual Bandits with Knapsack Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11383)
*Zhen Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带背包约束的上下文赌博机问题，提出了一种基于UCB算法的自然扩展，并结合再优化技术，实现了O((ln T)³/T)的平均遗憾上界，显著优于文献中基于再优化的动态定价问题的O(1/√T)界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带背包约束的上下文赌博机问题在许多实际场景中（如收益管理、资源分配）具有重要应用价值。现有基于再优化技术的动态定价问题仅能达到O(1/√T)的遗憾界，作者希望提出更优的算法以显著降低遗憾界，提高在线决策的效率。

Method: 提出UCB家族算法的自然且简单的扩展，核心思路是对每个到达的顾客进行线性收益函数的置信上界估计，并在资源容量约束下进行再优化（re-optimization）来确定当前的最优产品分配。再优化技术使得算法能够利用累积信息不断改进决策。

Result: 算法在水平长度为T的情况下实现了平均遗憾O((ln T)³/T)，该界显著优于文献中基于再优化的密切相关的动态定价问题所达到的O(1/√T)界。

Conclusion: 通过将UCB方法与再优化技术结合，可以在带背包约束的上下文赌博机问题中实现远优于传统方法的遗憾界，展示了再优化在在线学习问题中的强大潜力。

Abstract: We study new algorithms for Contextual Bandits with Knapsack. In these problems, there are finitely many types of customers, products, and resources. Each product is made from a fixed combination of resources, and resources have finite capacity. A decision maker must assign each arriving customer one out of a set of multiple possible products. Every assignment of a customer to a product will generate a random reward, which equals an unknown linear function of customer and product features, plus a noise term. The objective is to jointly learn the mean reward function, and to make online assignments to minimize the expected revenue loss relative to an optimal policy that knows the reward function. We propose a natural and simple extension of the Upper-Confidence-Bound (UCB) family of algorithms and apply re-optimization techniques. We show that by taking advantage of re-optimization, our algorithm achieves an average regret of $O(\frac{(\ln T)^3}{T})$ where $T$ is the horizon length. Our bound significantly reduces the $O(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}})$ bound in the literature for closely related dynamic-pricing problems that are based on re-optimization.

</details>


### [249] [Mechanism Design for Generative Engines: From Exploitation toward Win-Win Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11390)
*Chen Xu,Zitian Guo,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成式搜索引擎生态中内容提供者与平台之间因引文机制而引发的"引文战争"问题，提出了一种名为VCR的基于可验证内容奖励的平台-创作者机制，通过在三个基准上的实验验证其有效性与双赢效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式引擎将引文作为注意力、归属和下游价值分配的关键机制，导致内容提供者有动机优化模型引用而牺牲文档质量，平台则需维护答案质量与可信归属，二者之间存在战略张力且可能升级为引文战争。现有对抗性防御方法难以适应GEO攻击，需要新的机制来从根本上调和对立激励。

Method: 将供应商-平台交互形式化为一个具有部分观测的重复Stackelberg博弈；通过局部最优响应分析识别引文竞争趋近惰性静止结果的条件；在此基础上提出VCR机制——平台不仅惩罚可疑改写，还奖励能呈现可核查事实性内容的改写，从而将创作者激励与答案可信度对齐。实验在三个基准上进行，并与最强基线比较Net defense-utility得分。

Result: 在三个基准的实验中，VCR机制一致地取得最大的Net defense-utility分数，相比最强基线平均提升12.1个百分点，并在经验等价性准则下实现双赢结果。

Conclusion: 研究表明引文竞争可能陷入惰性静止的不利均衡，而VCR通过正向激励可核查事实内容不仅提升了防御效果，还实现了平台与创作者的双赢，为生成式搜索引擎生态中的引文治理提供了有效机制设计方向。

Abstract: Generative engines are reshaping the web ecosystem by making citations a key mechanism for allocating attention, attribution, and downstream value. This creates a strategic tension: content providers are incentivized to optimize for model citation, while platforms must preserve answer quality and trustworthy attribution. We show that this tension can escalate into citation wars. In repeated simulations, state-of-the-art generative engine optimization (GEO) attacks adapt to conventional defenses by producing citation-seeking rewrites that degrade document quality and introduce unsupported claims. To study this problem, we formulate the supplier--platform interaction as a repeated Stackelberg game with partial monitoring. A local best-response analysis identifies when citation competition approaches an inert stationary outcome. Motivated by this finding, we propose a platform--creator mechanism called VCR based on verifiable-content rewards. Rather than only penalizing suspicious rewrites, the platform also credits rewrites that surface checkable factual substance, aligning creator incentives with answer trustworthiness. Experiments on three benchmarks show that VCR consistently achieves the largest Net defense-utility score, outperforming the strongest baseline by an average of 12.1 percentage points, and produces a win--win outcome under our empirical equivalence criterion.

</details>


### [250] [Unmasking Toxic Mimicry in Medical Offline Reinforcement Learning for ICU Sepsis Management via Counterfactual Clinical Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11410)
*Hangqi Ren,Junyi Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实临床审计（CCA）框架，用于评估离线强化学习在ICU治疗决策中的安全性，发现标准评估指标（MSE和FQE）无法检测"毒性模仿"这一失败模式，并通过生理扰动测试揭示了MedDT模型在乳酸升高时反而降低血管加压素剂量、违反复苏指南的危险行为，而HCT-RL模型表现符合生理逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线强化学习评估指标（MSE和FQE）仅衡量行为模仿程度，无法识别agents复刻有害治疗模式（如舒适护理过渡期间撤药）的毒性模仿问题，导致统计拟合良好但临床不安全的模型被错误地视为可靠。需要一种能够检测临床安全性的审计评估框架。

Method: 基于MIMIC-III数据库，提出Counterfactual Clinical Audit（CCA）框架：通过对生理状态施加锚定于脓毒症生存运动（SSC）指南的扰动，压力测试RL智能体的行为响应。审计对象包括Medical Decision Transformer（MedDT）和Historical Causal Transformer（HCT-RL），后者结合了因果动作屏蔽、倾向性重要性加权和保守Q学习。

Result: CCA揭示MedDT在乳酸水平升高时反而降低血管加压素剂量，与复苏指南相矛盾；而HCT-RL能保持生理一致性的响应。这说明了统计拟合与临床安全之间的系统性错位。

Conclusion: 反事实审计应作为医学RL评估的必要标准，以弥补传统评估指标在检测临床不安全行为方面的不足。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) offers considerable promise for optimizing ICU treatment decisions, yet standard evaluation metrics Mean Squared Error (MSE) and Fitted Q-Evaluation (FQE) assess only behavioral imitation and cannot detect Toxic Mimicry, a failure mode in which agents replicate harmful patterns such as treatment withdrawal during comfort-care transitions. Using the MIMIC-III database, we propose the Counterfactual Clinical Audit (CCA) framework, which stress-tests RL agents through physiological perturbations anchored in Surviving Sepsis Campaign (SSC) guidelines. We audit a Medical Decision Transformer (MedDT) and a Historical Causal Transformer (HCT-RL), the latter employing Causal Action Shielding, propensity-based importance weighting, and Conservative Q-Learning. CCA reveals that MedDT paradoxically reduces vasopressor dosage as lactate escalates, contradicting resuscitation guidelines, while HCT-RL maintains physiologically consistent responses. These findings expose a systemic misalignment between statistical fit and clinical safety, supporting counterfactual audits as a necessary evaluation standard for medical RL.

</details>


### [251] [Diffusion-Based Data-Driven Assortment Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11419)
*Junyi Liao,Xiaohui Jiang,Zhengwei Tong,Ethan X. Fang,Vahid Tarokh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于引导离散扩散的模型无关同归优化框架，将同归表示为二进制向量，通过学习的逆向扩散过程进行随机搜索，并引入奖励引导机制来平衡探索与利用，在模型误设定下仍能鲁棒地恢复近最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统同归优化依赖参数化选择模型（如MNL及其变体），这些模型虽可处理但仍对模型误设定敏感，难以捕捉复杂顾客行为。需要一种模型无关、可扩展且鲁棒的求解方法。

Method: 将同归编码为二进制向量，采用引导离散扩散框架执行随机搜索：通过学习的逆向扩散过程生成候选同归；利用估计期望收益的奖励引导机制偏置局部转移，在生成过程中动态平衡探索与利用，避免显式组合枚举。

Result: 实证表明该方法能持续识别高质量同归，在模型误设定下保持鲁棒性，在高维场景中常能恢复近最优解；同时扩散的生成特性可产出多样化的高性能同归集合。

Conclusion: 生成建模有望成为数据驱动决策中组合优化的可扩展且鲁棒的新范式，超越了单一确定性解的限制，为同归优化提供了更灵活的求解途径。

Abstract: Assortment optimization is a fundamental problem in revenue management, typically addressed using parametric choice models such as the multinomial logit (MNL) and its variants. While these models enable tractable formulations, their performance is sensitive to model misspecification and often struggles to capture complex customer behavior. In this paper, we propose a model-agnostic framework for assortment optimization based on guided discrete diffusion. We represent assortments as binary vectors and perform stochastic search via a learned reverse diffusion process, avoiding explicit combinatorial enumeration. To incorporate decision objectives, we introduce a reward-guided mechanism that biases local transitions using estimates of expected revenue. This allows the method to effectively balance exploration and exploitation during generation. Empirically, we show that the proposed approach consistently identifies high-quality assortments and remains robust under model misspecification, often recovering near-optimal solutions in high-dimensional settings. Moreover, the generative nature of diffusion enables the production of diverse high-performing assortments, offering flexibility beyond a single deterministic solution. These results highlight the potential of generative modeling as a scalable and robust paradigm for combinatorial optimization in data-driven decision-making.

</details>


### [252] [Analysis of Federated Aggregation under Model Poisoning and Backdoor Attacks: A Reconstructed Cross-Dataset and Cross-Architecture Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11423)
*Soumya Mazumdar,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重建了一个500单元的联邦聚合方法评估矩阵，覆盖5种聚合方法（包括Trimmed Mean和Krum等）、5个数据集、5种架构及4种条件（干净、符号翻转、高斯、BadNets），通过审计执行日志和指标实现，确认了方法间的相对排名，同时指出BadNets指标实际为触发目标标签率（TTLR）而非传统攻击成功率，并揭露了FedPARETO实现中预测结果与聚合更新之间的潜在不一致。研究结论被明确限定为描述性比较，而非统计估计或普适性鲁棒性声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦聚合方法的鲁棒性比较需要同时考虑预测性能、威胁定义、指标语义和执行溯源，而现有的评估往往缺少对这些因素的系统性联合考量。本文旨在通过重建一个大规模的评估矩阵，并在严格的溯源审计基础上进行方法间的公正比较。

Method: 研究重建了一个500单元的seed-1评估矩阵，涵盖5种聚合方法、5个数据集、5种架构和4种条件。通过识别454个原始成功运行和36个修复/重跑执行，并对10个仅摘要溯源支持的SVHN单元格进行标记。对BadNets指标实现进行了审计以确认触发语义，同时对FedPARETO脚手架进行了代码审计以识别潜在的不一致路径。

Result: Trimmed Mean在干净条件下取得最高宏平均准确率（76.02%）和最低平均任务内排名（1.70）；Krum在符号翻转和高斯配置下均取得最高记录准确率。限制到21对均有原始成功日志的任务对时，相对排名不变。但审计发现BadNets指标实际为TTLR而非传统目标排除攻击成功率，且FedPARETO存在预测结果与聚合更新不一致的潜在路径。

Conclusion: 本研究结果为联邦聚合方法在特定配置下的比较提供了可追溯的经验证据，但鉴于种子单一、攻击与配置溯源不完整，以及指标实现中发现的偏差，结论应被理解为描述性比较而非统计估计或普适性的鲁棒性声明。

Abstract: Robust comparisons of federated aggregation methods require joint consideration of predictive performance, threat definitions, metric semantics, and execution provenance. A 500-cell seed-1 evaluation matrix was reconstructed across five aggregation methods, five datasets, five architectures, and four recorded conditions: clean, sign-flipping, Gaussian, and BadNets. Successful execution logs were identified for 454 original runs and 36 repaired or rerun executions, whereas 10 clean SVHN cells were supported by summary-only provenance. Trimmed Mean achieved the highest clean macro-mean accuracy (76.02%) and the lowest mean within-task rank (1.70). Krum attained the highest recorded accuracy under both sign-flipping and Gaussian configurations. These relative rankings remained unchanged when analysis was restricted to 21 task pairs for which original successful logs were available for every method-condition combination. Audit of the supplied BadNets metric implementation established that every test input is triggered prior to target-label counting; consequently, the retained metric represents Triggered Target-Label Rate (TTLR) rather than a conventional target-excluding attack success rate. An audit of the supplied FedPARETO scaffold further identified a pathway in which predictive summaries may characterize an uncorrupted local model while the aggregation weight is applied to a separately corrupted update, introducing a potential discrepancy between reported predictive outcomes and the updates used for aggregation. The canonical matrix contains a single identified seed for each cell, and exact attack and configuration lineage is incomplete. Accordingly, the findings should be interpreted as descriptive comparisons within the recorded configurations and not as statistical estimates or universal claims regarding robustness.

</details>


### [253] [Click2Poly: A VLM for vector mapping buildings and walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11424)
*Nicolas Girard,Jawher Ben Abdallah,Arno Gobbin,Liuyun Duan,Sacha Lepretre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Click2Poly是一个基于Florence-2视觉语言模型的人机协同AI助手，以QGIS插件形式实现，通过用户点击直接编辑建筑和墙体矢量图层，加速地理空间中建筑与墙体矢量提取的人工质量控制与修正流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑和墙体的精确矢量映射对地理空间应用至关重要，但现有深度学习方法虽能自动提取，仍需人工进行质量控制和修正复杂案例，这一人工步骤耗时费力，亟需加速。

Method: 扩展Florence-2视觉语言模型(VLM)，使模型能够响应用户点击并直接编辑建筑或墙体矢量图层，并以QGIS插件形式集成到实际生产环境中。

Result: 在真实生产环境中，Click2Poly作为QGIS插件成功加速了建筑和墙体矢量图层的人工编辑过程。

Conclusion: Click2Poly证明了将视觉语言模型与人机协同交互结合，可以有效加速地理空间矢量编辑工作流，是一种实用的生产级AI辅助工具。

Abstract: Accurate vector mapping of buildings and walls is critical for geospatial applications but remains a labor-intensive process. While recent deep learning methods have improved automatic extraction, in order to meet cartographic standards they always require a human to perform quality control and fix complex cases in the extraction. We present Click2Poly, a human-in-the-loop AI assistant designed to speed up this manual step. Extending the Florence-2 Vision Language Model (VLM), Click2Poly responds to user clicks by editing the building or wall vector layer directly. Implemented as a QGIS plugin, Click2Poly speeds up the manual editing of building and wall vector layers in a real-world production environment.

</details>


### [254] [Three Tokens Force Exponential Feature Rank in Nonnegative Kernel Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11427)
*Vicente Opazo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明，在最小内积（Min-IP）布尔输入任务上，全注意力与核注意力存在指数级表达能力差距：对于包含三个token的序列，任何单一归一化非负核注意力头要达到低于1/2的误差需要2^{Ω(m)}个特征，而密集softmax注意力仅需m维分数和恒定温度即可解决同一任务。此外，对有限字母表的多头多层草图模型证明了与独立答案数线性相关、与字母表大小对数相关的转录本下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全注意力和核注意力之间的根本表达能力差异，特别是在序列长度增长时核注意力压缩为固定维度草图这一机制带来的性能瓶颈，为注意力机制的理论分析和设计提供依据。

Method: 通过Min-IP布尔任务构造竞争性候选场景，对比rank-one归一化核注意力与密集softmax注意力的表达能力；利用信息论方法分析有限字母表的多头多层草图中的转录本下界。

Result: 在长度≤2的序列上核注意力可精确求解，但处理所有三token序列且误差<1/2时，单个归一化非负核注意力头需要2^{Ω(m)}个特征（即使允许任意有限维token值和任意查询相关的仿射读出）；softmax只需m维分数和常数温度。该结论在位置相关token映射和因果最终查询下依然成立。随着上下文增长，下界趋近精确的2^m特征实现。确定性编辑模型转录本下界为线性于独立答案数、对数于字母表大小。

Conclusion: 核注意力与全注意力之间存在指数的表达能力鸿沟，其根源在于核注意力将序列压缩为固定维度草图，在序列中出现竞争候选时会造成指数级的信息损失。位置相关映射和因果查询无法消除这一差距。

Abstract: Full attention exposes every token pair, whereas kernel attention compresses a sequence into a fixed-dimensional sketch. We show that this distinction becomes exponential at the first context length containing two competing candidates. On Min-IP over Boolean inputs, rank-one normalized kernel attention solves every sequence of length at most two exactly. In contrast, any single normalized nonnegative kernel-attention head that succeeds on all three-token sequences with error strictly below $1/2$ requires $2^{Ω(m)}$ features, even with arbitrary finite-dimensional tokenwise values and an arbitrary query-dependent affine readout. Dense softmax solves the same task with $m$-dimensional scores and constant temperature. The conclusion survives position-dependent token maps and a causal final query. As context length grows, the lower bound approaches the exact $2^m$-feature realization. Separately, for deterministic multihead, multilayer sketch models whose cross-token channels have finite alphabets, we prove a transcript lower bound linear in the number of independent answers and logarithmic in their alphabet size.

</details>


### [255] [AutoGrable: What Is a Good Graph for a Table?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11431)
*Tamara Cucumides,Floris Geerts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoGrable，一个无需训练图模型的表格/关系数据库到图构造准则与方法。其核心洞见是：在最小化"行即节点"的抽象下，1-WL受限的GNN只能感知行的颜色精化划分，因此好的图构造应使不同标签的行被划分开、相同标签的行不被拆分。AutoGrable据此将图构造简化为列选择问题，用标签对齐风险分数（无需物化图或训练GNN）贪心搜索候选子集，适用于单表与外键模式。实验表明它能在候选图中保留最优者、在受控任务中恢复标签生成列、在真实任务上优于固定/随机/任务感知构造器，且是唯一能主动选择不建图的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格和关系数据库本身没有图，应用GNN前需要人为或启发式地决定如何构建图（哪些实体作节点、如何连边）。现有的做法要么依赖人工/模式启发式，要么需在每个候选图上训练模型后择优，成本高昂。作者希望给出一个无需训练图模型的准则，从而高效且可判定地挑选最佳图构造。

Method: 以"每行一个节点"的最小表到图抽象为基础，利用1-WL界限制了GNN的区分能力：一个构造的好坏等价于颜色精化类对行的划分质量。AutoGrable将图构造转化为列选择问题（对关联构造，划分由所选列固定），并提出一个标签对齐风险分数：在所选列划分的每个块上取最常值预测器的留出风险，并加入占用惩罚项以衡量块是否过薄。该分数不需要物化图或训练GNN，因此可对子集空间进行廉价贪心搜索，同时适用于单表和外键模式。

Result: 实验显示：在候选图空间中，所提分数能丢弃大量候选而仅保留最优者；在受控任务上AutoGrable能恢复生成标签的列；在真实任务上配合固定预测器时优于固定、随机和任务感知的构造器；且它是对比方法中唯一能够主动放弃建图（当任何图都无帮助时）的方案。

Conclusion: AutoGrable提供了一种无需训练图模型即可评估并构造图表征的实用准则，将图构造与预测任务解耦，实现了高效、可判定的图构建，并能理性地拒绝无益的图构造。

Abstract: Graph learning presupposes a graph, and tables and relational databases do not come with one. Applying a GNN to them requires deciding which entities become nodes, which of them to connect, and through which relations---a decision made by hand, by schema heuristics, or by training a model on every candidate graph and keeping the best. We give a criterion that requires no trained graph model. In the minimal table-to-graph abstraction each row is a node, so a message-passing GNN, bounded by 1-WL, sees a construction only as a partition of the rows into colour-refinement classes: a construction is good for a task when that partition separates rows with different labels and does not split rows that share one. AutoGrable turns this criterion into a construction procedure. For incidence constructions the partition is fixed by the selected columns, so building a graph reduces to choosing them, and we score a candidate subset by a label-alignment risk: the held-out risk of the best predictor constant on its blocks, penalised by an occupancy term measuring how thinly the blocks are populated. The score materialises no graph and trains no GNN, so AutoGrable can search the space of subsets greedily and cheaply, and returns the resulting grable for single tables and for foreign-key schemas alike. Our experiments show that over a space of candidate graphs the score discards a large fraction while retaining the best; that AutoGrable recovers the columns that generate the label on controlled tasks and outperforms fixed, random, and task-aware constructors on real tasks under a fixed predictor; and that it is the only method compared that can decline to build a graph when none helps.

</details>


### [256] [Variational Parameter Calibration with Physics-Aware Latent-Space Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11435)
*Qiyao Zhou,Xujia Zhu,Pierre Joli,Yu Cong,Sibo Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理感知神经网络和潜空间表示的降阶代理框架，用于参数化动力系统的前向建模与变分参数估计。该方法通过自编码器构建可微代理模型，将物理参数映射到预测流场，并结合可观测监督训练与潜空间参数反演，在两个CFD基准上验证了其在噪声、低分辨率、随机遮挡和分块部分观测等现实场景下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化动力系统的前向与逆向建模需要代理模型不仅能在状态预测上保持精度，还要能为参数标定提供有效信息。然而，目前缺乏一种系统性的端到端可微公式来耦合基于深度学习的降阶代理与变分参数估计，标准代理模型在逆向建模中因缺乏端到端可微性或物理感知而导致校准性能不足。

Method: 提出一种基于自编码器的潜空间降阶框架：(1) 离线训练阶段，通过可观测监督（observable supervision）约束潜变量保留与系统参数相关的信息；(2) 构建从物理参数到预测流场的可微代理映射；(3) 在线逆向问题通过代理诱导的观测算子（surrogate-induced observation operator）在参数空间中求解变分估计问题。

Result: 在两个CFD基准上的结果表明：(1) 仅依靠重建精度不足以支撑逆向建模；(2) 可观测监督改善了潜表示在案例层面的可分性与时间组织性；(3) 在含噪、低分辨率、随机遮挡和分块部分观测等现实测量场景下，所提框架相比标准代理模型普遍降低了标定误差和变异性。

Conclusion: 该工作系统性地弥合了深度学习降阶代理与变分参数估计之间的端到端可微性缺口，证明物理感知的可观测监督训练能显著提升逆向参数标定的准确性与稳健性，为参数化动力系统的双向（前向-逆向）建模提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Forward and inverse modeling of parametric dynamical systems requires surrogate models that are not only accurate for state prediction, but also informative for parameter calibration. However, a systematic end-to-end differentiable formulation for coupling deep-learning-based reduced-order surrogates with variational parameter estimation remains underdeveloped. In this work, we introduce a physics-aware neural-network-based latent-space framework for reduced-order forward modeling and variational parameter estimation. The proposed autoencoder-based approach yields a differentiable surrogate that maps physical parameters to predicted flow fields through a latent representation. The observable supervision is used during offline training to encourage the latent variables to retain information correlated with system parameters, while the online inverse problem is solved in the parameter space through the surrogate-induced observation operator. The method is evaluated on two computational-fluid-dynamics benchmarks. The results show that reconstruction accuracy alone is insufficient for inverse modeling, owing to the lack of end-to-end differentiability or physics awareness for variational parameter calibration. Quantitative latent-space analysis further shows that observable supervision improves case-level separability and temporal organization of latent representations. Experiments with realistic measurement settings, including noisy, low-resolution, randomly masked, and block-wise partial observations, demonstrate the robustness of the proposed framework and show that it generally reduces calibration error and variability compared with the standard surrogate models.

</details>


### [257] [XGBoost "is all you need": the case of forecasting transmitted heat energy in District Heating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11446)
*Milan Zdravković*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了XGBoost和LSTM两种方法在区域供热系统（DHS）中传输热能预测的性能。实验表明，XGBoost在此任务中始终优于LSTM，原因在于LSTM在数据可用性较少的区间会产生更大的误差。此外，传统机器学习方法（XGBoost）具有更低的计算成本，既能节省开支，又能减少数据分析任务相关的碳足迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在时间序列预测场景下，传统机器学习算法何时优于深度学习网络，并评估其在计算成本和环境影响方面的优势。

Method: 在真实世界区域供热系统数据集上，对XGBoost和LSTM两种方法进行对比实验，通过误差分布分析解释两者性能差异的原因。

Result: XGBoost在区供系统热能预测任务中始终优于LSTM；LSTM在数据较稀疏的区间产生更显著的误差。

Conclusion: 传统机器学习方法（如XGBoost）在特定时间序列预测任务中可优于深度学习模型，同时更低的计算需求能带来成本节省和碳减排的双重效益。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative study of two distinct approaches, XGBoost and Long-Short Term Memory (LSTM), for forecasting transmitted heat energy in District Heating Systems (DHS). The objective is to explore scenarios in which conventional ML algorithms demonstrate better performance over deep learning networks in time series forecasting and the associated benefits in terms of computational cost and environmental impact. The study focuses on a real-world DHS dataset. Through experimentation and analysis, it is demonstrated that XGBoost consistently outperforms LSTM in this specific forecasting task. The difference is explained by the error distribution illustrating that LSTM makes more significant errors in the intervals of less data availability. The reduced computational demands of conventional ML approaches not only result in cost savings but also minimize the carbon footprint associated with data analysis tasks in energy systems.

</details>


### [258] [PAC-Bayes Beyond Parameter Space: Behavioral Equivalence, Z-Information, and Exact Complexity Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11465)
*Vasant G. Honavar,Satish Kumar Keshri,Neil Ashtekar,Zehao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种精确的结构化分解框架，将经典PAC-Bayes KL散度分解为行为选择项和实现层级项（Z-information），论证了预测行为而非内部实现才是PAC-Bayes复杂度的自然对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典PAC-Bayes理论通过控制后验与先验之间的KL散度来提供泛化保证，但预测风险只依赖于假设诱导的预测行为，而非实现该行为的内部配置。在过参数化系统中，大量不同的配置可以产生完全相同的预测行为，然而经典KL散度无法区分行为层面的不确定性与行为等价实现之间的变化，导致复杂度的计算包含了不必要的实现级冗余。

Method: 作者通过可测行为映射形式化行为等价性，利用测度分解（measure disintegration）将配置空间上的概率测度分解为预测行为上的分布和行为纤维上的条件分布。由此将经典PAC-Bayes KL散度精确分解为两个部分：(1) 行为选择项（behavior-selection term），刻画预测行为层面的不确定性；(2) 实现层级项（realization-level term），即纤维内期望条件KL，其负值被定义为Z-information。此外，行为选择项被证明具有变分特征化：它是在所有诱导相同预测行为分布的后续中KL散度的最小值，由纤维对称化的典型代表达到。

Result: 作者得到了经典PAC-Bayes KL散度的精确结构分解，定义了Z-information作为KL散度与纯预测行为复杂度之间的精确间隙；证明了行为选择项的精确变分特征化及其由纤维对称化后验达到的最小性；并展示了对称性、行为保持方向、纤维几何以及与纤维保持扰动的不变性都可以从同一行为映射结构中自然涌现。

Conclusion: 预测行为应被视为PAC-Bayes复杂度的自然测量对象。经典KL散度混入了实现层级的冗余信息，而通过行为映射分解后，可以精确分离出行为层面的复杂度（行为选择项）与实现层面的贡献（Z-information所度量的间隙），这为过参数化系统中更精确的泛化界提供了理论基础。

Abstract: PAC-Bayes theory provides generalization guarantees by controlling the Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence between posterior and prior distributions over a chosen hypothesis representation. However, predictive risk depends only on the predictive behavior induced by a hypothesis, not on the particular internal realization that implements that behavior. In over-parameterized systems, many distinct configurations induce identical predictive behavior, yet the classical PAC-Bayes KL divergence does not distinguish uncertainty over predictive behavior from variation among behaviorally equivalent realizations.
  We show that this distinction induces an exact structural decomposition of classical PAC-Bayes complexity. We formalize behavioral equivalence through a measurable behavior map and use measure disintegration to decompose probability measures on the configuration space into a distribution over predictive behaviors and conditional distributions over behavioral fibers. This yields an exact decomposition of the classical PAC-Bayes KL divergence into a behavior-selection term and a realization-level term given by an expected conditional KL within fibers.
  We define Z-information as the negative of this realization-level contribution: the exact gap between the KL divergence and the complexity of uncertainty over predictive behavior alone. We further show that the behavior-selection term admits an exact variational characterization: it is the minimum KL divergence among all posteriors inducing the same distribution over predictive behaviors, attained by a canonical fiber-symmetrized representative.
  Finally, we show that symmetry, behavior-preserving directions, fiber geometry, and invariance under fiber-preserving perturbations arise naturally from the same behavior-map structure. Together, these results identify predictive behavior as the natural object of PAC-Bayes complexity.

</details>


### [259] [Dual-Primal Graph VAEs for Noisy Label Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11473)
*Patrick Stinson,Nikolaus Kriegeskorte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图变分自编码器（Graph VAE）的众包噪声标签推断方法，通过GAT消息传递在众包数据集的邻接图和其对偶图上分别进行编码与解码，将真实标签作为潜在变量，实现无需伪标签或合成数据的无监督表示学习，并在众包基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从众包噪声标签中推断真实标签是重要的理论与实践问题。现有方法中，经典贝叶斯模型需要指定特定的生成模型族进行推断，而基于神经网络的方法要么仍依赖较为简单的生成模型，要么需要伪标签或合成数据来训练聚合分类器，缺乏一种既能充分利用神经网络表达能力又无需额外标签数据的统一方案。

Method: 提出一个图VAE架构：解码器与编码器分别使用基于GAT（图注意力网络）的消息传递机制，在众包数据集的邻接图及其对偶图上运行。真实标签作为潜在变量建模，从而在不训练独立分类器的情况下实现无监督表示学习。此外，原始众包图可扩展整合辅助信息（如基于噪声标签训练的神经网络分类器表示），以提升测试时分类性能。

Result: 该模型在众包基准数据集上达到了最先进的性能；通过将神经网络分类器的表示作为辅助信息注入原始众包图，能够显著提升分类器在测试阶段的分类表现，验证了方法的通用性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: Graph VAE框架为众包标签聚合提供了一种灵活的替代方案，既摆脱了贝叶斯模型对生成模型族假设的依赖，又避免了伪标签或合成数据的需要。图的增强能力使该方法能够自然融合多种辅助信息，展现出广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Inferring the ground-truth from noisy crowdsourced labels is an important theoretical and practical problem. Neural network-based methods offer an alternative to classical Bayesian models which require specifying a family of generative models used for inference. However, current models either still rely on fairly simple generative models for inference or require pseudo-labels or synthetic data to train the aggregate classifier. We propose a graph VAE architecture in which the decoder and encoder use GAT-based message passing on the adjacency graph of a crowdsourced dataset and its dual, respectively. The ground-truth labels are treated as latent variables, enabling unsupervised representation learning without needing to train a separate classifier. We show our model achieves state of the art performance on crowdsourcing benchmarks. We then demonstrate the generality of our approach by showing how the original crowdsourcing graph can be augmented to incorporate side information such as representations from neural network classifiers trained on the noisy labels to substantially boost their classification performance at test time.

</details>


### [260] [Convergence Guarantees of Gradient Descent for Neural Networks via Generalized Lipschitz Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11479)
*Siqiao Mu,Diego Klabjan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为任意宽度或深度的前馈神经网络在无特殊初始化或数据集要求下，证明了梯度下降的收敛性保证。通过引入一种新的广义Lipschitz光滑条件，作者证明L层神经网络的梯度范数平方最小值以O(1/T^{1/L})的速率收敛到零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络收敛性分析通常依赖特殊初始化方案、特定数据集或窄/浅网络假设，缺乏对一般前馈网络在任意宽度、任意深度下梯度下降收敛性的统一理论保证。

Method: 关键理论洞见在于激活函数的Lipschitz性质在反复复合后仍被部分保留，由此导出一种广义Lipschitz光滑性条件——梯度的变化被参数空间的变化乘以参数范数的多项式项所上界约束。该条件对模型函数和损失函数均成立，进而推导出下降引理：只要学习率相对于参数范数足够小，损失就会下降。通过确保参数范数不会过快增长至无穷，最终证明收敛率。

Result: 证明了L层前馈神经网络中，梯度平方范数的最小值在T次迭代中以O(1/T^{1/L})的速率收敛到零。

Conclusion: 仅需激活函数满足Lipschitz光滑、Lipschitz连续且线性有界（如linear、tanh、softplus、sigmoid），损失函数满足输出层面的Lipschitz光滑（如均方误差），即可保证梯度下降的收敛性，无需对初始化或数据集做特殊假设。

Abstract: We establish convergence guarantees of gradient descent for general feedforward neural networks of arbitrary width or depth, with no special requirements on the initialization or dataset. We only assume that the activation functions are Lipschitz smooth, Lipschitz continuous, and linearly bounded--- properties that hold for linear, tanh, softplus, and sigmoid activation functions. For the loss function, we require that it is Lipschitz smooth in the model outputs, which is true for mean-squared error. The key theoretical insight is that the Lipschitz properties of the activation functions are partially preserved even through repeated compositions, leading to a novel generalized Lipschitz smoothness condition where the change in gradient is upper bounded by the change in the parameter space, multiplied by polynomial terms of the parameter norms at both endpoints. This type of condition holds for both the model function and the loss function, enabling a descent lemma where the loss decreases as long as the learning rate is small enough with respect to the parameter norms. By ensuring that the parameter norms do not grow too quickly to infinity, we prove that the minimum squared gradient norm converges to zero in $T$ iterations at rate $O(1/T^{1/L})$ for an $L$-layer neural network.

</details>


### [261] [Defending against Model Extraction for GNNs with Model Reprogramming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11495)
*Yan Wen,Zhenyi Wang,Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GraphRP，一种基于图重编程（Model Reprogramming）的主动防御框架，用于保护GNN知识图谱中的机器学习即服务（MLaaS）模型免遭模型提取（ME）攻击。通过结构感知门控机制和可学习的拓扑原型，在不牺牲良性查询保真度的前提下，显著削弱对抗查询的模型提取攻击效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN防御方法存在"欧几里得偏差"——直接迁移图像领域的随机噪声等策略到图领域，忽略节点间的复杂拓扑依赖关系，导致严重的效用退化；而水印等被动防御无法实时阻止模型窃取。需要一种针对图结构特性的主动防御方案。

Method: 提出GraphRP框架，核心包含：(1) 结构感知门控机制，由可学习的拓扑原型驱动，形成动态"结构防火墙"；(2) 对良性查询保持训练流形上的保真度，对对抗查询沿扰动方向最大化Fisher信息量；(3) 在标准假设下（有界损失、最优攻击者、局部二阶近似）证明攻击者估计误差的下界随重编程噪声的结构敏感性增加而增大。

Result: 在硬标签和软标签模型提取攻击上进行了大量实验，GraphRP显著降低了攻击有效性，同时保持了良性查询的效用。

Conclusion: GraphRP通过结构感知的动态门控机制解决了GNN模型提取防御中"欧几里得偏差"问题，提供了理论保障与实验验证相结合的主动防御方案，在防御效果和效用保持之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) serve as the backbone for high-stakes applications in Machine-Learning-as-a-Service (MLaaS). Still, their black-box deployment exposes them to Model Extraction (ME) attacks, in which adversaries steal intellectual property by querying APIs. Existing defenses suffer from a critical ''Euclidean bias'': they transfer image-based strategies (e.g., random noise) to graphs, ignoring the complex topological dependencies between nodes, which often results in severe utility degradation. Passive methods like watermarking also fail to prevent theft in real time. To bridge this gap, we propose GraphRP (Graph Reprogramming Protection), a proactive defense framework that repurposes Model Reprogramming for security. Unlike static perturbations, GraphRP introduces a Structure-Aware Gating Mechanism driven by learnable topological prototypes. This creates a dynamic ''structural firewall'' that selectively modulates the model's decision boundary: it preserves fidelity for benign queries residing on the training manifold, while maximizing the Fisher Information along the perturbation direction for adversarial queries. Under standard assumptions (bounded loss, optimal attacker, and local second-order approximation), we prove a lower bound on the attacker's estimation error that increases with the structural sensitivity of the reprogramming noise. Extensive experiments on both hard-label and soft-label ME attacks demonstrate that GraphRP significantly degrades attack effectiveness while preserving benign utility.

</details>


### [262] [HyperFix: Combinatorial Nonlinear Correction for Task Vector Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11499)
*Hyo Seo Kim,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperFix，一种轻量级超网络，用于预测与任务子集条件相关的权重空间非线性修正，解决任务向量合并中可变子集的问题。HyperFix在单例、两个和三个任务的子集上训练一次，即可泛化到更大子集，无需逐子集优化，在多个基准上优于现有方法并降低调参成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务向量合并方法通常针对特定子集进行标量调参，当合并的子集发生变化时需要重复调参，且仅支持线性缩放，限制了合并的灵活性和效率。因此需要一种能够适应任意子集组合的无重复调参的合并方法。

Method: 将跨可变任务子集的合并问题形式化为组合修正问题，引入HyperFix——一种轻量级超网络，在权重空间中预测子集条件下的非线性修正。先在任务库的单例、成对和三元子集上训练，再泛化到更大的子集。通过局部扰动分析来界定线性合并之外的残差修正，并启发从小任务更新中学习该修正。

Result: 在多种基准实验上，HyperFix在所有任务向量合并方法中取得了最优性能，同时显著降低了调参成本。

Conclusion: HyperFix通过一次性训练实现对任意任务子集合并的非线性修正预测，突破了传统标量调参在子集可变场景下的局限，为任务向量合并提供了一种高效且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Task vectors enable model merging without joint retraining. In practice, the subset of task vectors to be merged may vary, but many existing methods use scalar tuning for a particular subset, requiring repeated tuning across subsets and restricting task vector merging to linear rescaling. We therefore formulate merging across varying task subsets as a combinatorial correction problem and introduce HyperFix, a lightweight hypernetwork that predicts subset-conditioned nonlinear corrections in weight space. Trained once on singleton, pair, and triple subsets from a task bank, HyperFix generalizes to larger subsets without per-subset optimization. Our local perturbation analysis bounds the residual correction beyond linear merging and motivates learning it from small task updates. Experiments across diverse benchmarks show that HyperFix outperforms existing task vector merging methods while reducing tuning cost.

</details>


### [263] [RelShap: Relationally Consistent Shapley Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11508)
*Seungeun Lee,Joao Fonseca,Julia Stoyanovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RelShap是一个将关系约束和数据溯源融入Shapley值计算的框架，通过将背景数据和联盟评估限制在关系上有效的配置中，解决传统特征归因方法因丢弃结构约束和特征独立假设而产生的误导性解释问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习pipeline通常将关系数据展平为单表表示，丢弃了结构约束。而广为使用的基于Shapley值的特征归因依赖于特征独立性假设，会在底层数据中不可能出现的组合上评估模型，从而产生误导性解释。

Method: 提出RelShap框架，将关系约束和数据溯源融入Shapley值计算，同时限制背景数据和联盟评估为关系上有效的配置。该框架与估计器无关，可与Kernel SHAP、Monte Carlo和Leverage SHAP组合而不改变其采样或加权性质。此外，利用函数依赖在特征联盟上诱导出的等价类来减少运行时间，并提供了预期加速的组合特征描述。

Result: 在多个数据集、模型和估计器上的实验表明，RelShap产生的解释更忠实于数据生成过程，在受控设置中能正确识别主导特征，而现有方法（包括Conditional SHAP和ManifoldShap）无法做到。

Conclusion: RelShap通过保留关系结构约束来提升Shapley值归因的忠实度，同时利用函数依赖等价类优化运行时效率，是一种更符合数据生成过程的特征归因方法。

Abstract: Machine learning pipelines commonly flatten relational data into single-table representations, discarding structural constraints. Widely used Shapley value-based feature attributions then rely on feature independence, evaluating the model on combinations that could never arise in the underlying data, producing misleading explanations. We propose RelShap, a framework that incorporates relational constraints and data provenance into Shapley value computation, restricting both background data and coalition evaluation to relationally valid configurations. The framework is estimator-agnostic and composes with Kernel SHAP, Monte Carlo, and Leverage SHAP without altering their sampling or weighting properties. Functional dependencies further induce equivalence classes over feature coalitions, which RelShap exploits to reduce runtime without changing Shapley values; we provide a combinatorial characterization of the expected speedup. Experiments across multiple datasets, models, and estimators show that RelShap produces explanations that are more faithful to the data-generating process, correctly identifying the dominant feature in controlled settings where existing methods, including Conditional SHAP and ManifoldShap, do not. Our code is available at: https://github.com/duneag2/relshap.

</details>


### [264] [Let it Cook: Learning to Wait in Sequential Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11511)
*Christopher Watson,Arjun Krishna,Dinesh Jayaraman,Rajeev Alur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"学习等待"框架，训练智能体在顺序决策中主动暂停感知与控制，以减少资源消耗而不牺牲任务性能，并通过字典序多目标强化学习实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体通常需要在每个时间步都进行感知和决策，但在许多任务（如煮咖啡）中存在环境可以自然演化的阶段，此时持续监控并非必要。通过减少不必要的感知和决策，可以节省资源或将注意力转向其他任务。

Method: 形式化为在保持任务性能（如总完成任务时间）的前提下最小化感知和决策频率的目标；提出训练"等待策略"的方法，采用具有字典序（lexicographically）排序目标的多目标强化学习来优化等待行为。

Result: 在4个离散状态的家居任务和3个连续状态环境中，方法成功学习了等待行为，并能将预训练策略适配为在适当位置等待；不同任务允许不同等待量，但方法一致地找到包含大量等待的解决方案，有时等待时间超过任务总时长的50%。

Conclusion: 学习等待是可行且有效的，能够显著减少智能体的感知和决策频率而不牺牲任务性能，为资源受限场景下的顺序决策提供了新的优化维度。

Abstract: In sequential decision making, an agent typically observes its environment and acts at every timestep. However, such active participation may not always be necessary; tasks such as brewing coffee include periods that are served equally well by letting the environment evolve without constant monitoring and control. During such periods, the agent could simply wait to conserve its resources, or redirect its attention to another task. We capitalize on these opportunities by training a "waiting policy" that decides where and how long to wait. This involves forgoing sensing to commit to a wait action, representing a deliberate pause for a set number of timesteps. We formalize "learning to wait" as minimizing the frequency of sensing and decision making without sacrificing task performance (e.g., the total amount of time to complete a task). To train a waiting policy, we propose an approach that employs reinforcement learning with lexicographically ordered objectives. In experiments across 4 discrete-state household tasks and 3 continuous-state environments, we show that our approach successfully learns waiting behaviors, and can adapt pre-trained policies to wait where appropriate. While different tasks permit different amounts of waiting without sacrificing task performance, our approach consistently finds solutions with significant waiting, sometimes waiting for over 50 percent of the task duration.

</details>


### [265] [FLARE++: Low-rank attention with dynamic attention routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11519)
*Vedant Puri,Yongjie Jessica Zhang,Levent Burak Kara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLARE++ 是一种低秩注意力架构，通过动态令牌路由替代 FLARE 中固定的学习查询模板，在保持 O(NM) 线性复杂度的同时，在 PDE 代理基准上相比 FLARE 平均提升 24% 准确率，并支持多 GPU 上下文并行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLARE 这类高效潜注意力模型将所有 N 个令牌路由到 M 个学习到的潜查询，但这些查询是固定参数模板，训练后对所有输入都相同，缺乏对具体输入的自适应性，限制了模型表达能力。

Method: FLARE++ 复用 FLARE 自身的编码器来构建路由查询：用学习到的潜种子驱动一次额外的编码调用，将 N 个输入令牌聚合成 M 个与输入条件相关的查询，再由这些查询决定令牌的压缩与再分配。整个路由过程仅由标准缩放点积注意力（SDPA）调用组成，保持显式低秩分解和 O(NM) 线性复杂度。同时提供多 GPU 上下文并行实现，将输入令牌分片到不同设备，无需在单设备上聚合完整令牌序列。

Result: 在多个标准 PDE 代理基准上，FLARE++ 表现具有竞争力，相比固定查询的 FLARE 平均提升 24% 准确率；在 Long Range Arena 基准上平均准确率提升 2.3 个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过将静态学习查询替换为输入条件化查询，FLARE++ 在保持线性复杂度与显式低秩路由优势的同时显著提升了模型精度，证明动态路由策略比固定查询模板更有效，且该设计可高效扩展到多 GPU 场景。

Abstract: Full self-attention is a strong token mixer for PDE surrogates on irregular domains, but its quadratic cost limits its use on high-resolution problems. Efficient latent-attention models such as the Fast Low-rank Attention Routing Engine (FLARE) avoid that cost by routing all N tokens through M << N learned latent queries, but those queries are parameters: once trained, the same learned query templates serve every input. We remove this restriction with FLARE++, a low-rank attention architecture with dynamic token routing. FLARE++ reuses FLARE's own encoder to build its routing queries: learned latent seeds drive one extra encode call that gathers the N input tokens into M input-conditioned queries, and those queries then determine how the same tokens are compressed and redistributed. This preserves FLARE's explicit low-rank factorization and linear O(NM) complexity, and expresses the complete routing operation with standard scaled dot-product attention (SDPA) calls alone. We also provide a multi-GPU context-parallel implementation that shards input tokens across devices without ever gathering the full token sequence on one of them. FLARE++ is competitive across a set of standard PDE surrogate benchmarks, improving on fixed-query FLARE by 24% on average, and it gains 2.3 points of average accuracy on Long Range Arena.

</details>


### [266] [Hierarchical Federated Transfer Learning in Digital Twin-Based Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11532)
*Qasim Zia,Saide Zhu,Haoxin Wang,Zafar Iqbal,Yingshu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于数字孪生车载自组网（DT-VANET）的分层联邦迁移学习（HFTL）框架，通过按车辆类型聚类并结合联邦迁移学习来解决数据异构性和数据稀疏性问题，同时引入基于数据质量评分的机制来抵御恶意车辆攻击，在真实数据集上验证了算法的有效性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在DT-VANET中虽然能提供数据隐私保护，但在面对车辆间数据异构性和数据稀疏性时难以训练出高质量的全局模型，导致对不同类型车辆的预测精度不理想。现有方法未能有效解决这一问题。

Method: 1) 结合联邦迁移学习（FTL），按车辆类型进行聚类；2) 提出分层联邦迁移学习（HFTL）框架；3) 构建DT-VANET框架，设计两个算法分别用于云服务器模型更新和簇内联邦迁移学习；4) 设计基于数据质量评分的机制以防止恶意车辆影响全局模型。

Result: 在真实数据集上进行了详细实验，使用多种性能指标验证了所提算法的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的HFTL框架能够有效应对数据异构性和数据稀疏性挑战，提升全局模型对不同车辆类型的预测精度，同时通过数据质量评分机制增强了系统的安全性。

Abstract: In recent research on the Digital Twin-based Vehicular Ad hoc Network(DT-VANET), Federated Learning (FL) has shown its ability to provide data privacy. However, Federated learning struggles to adequately train a global model when confronted with data heterogeneity and data sparsity among vehicles, which ensure suboptimal accuracy in making precise predictions for different vehicle types.
